# DAPR Module Folder Rename Map

**Date:** 2026-09-23

Every folder under `Classes/` and `Canvas Links/` is now named after a Canvas module, taken from the module list in that course's current cartridge. Every file was placed in the module whose page actually references it. Files no page references went to `_unplaced/`, keeping the folder name they came from. Nothing was deleted.

**Updated 2026-09-23 (evening).** The right column is where each file is **now**, after the numbering strip, the `_unplaced` to `_unused` rename, the rescue passes, the placement of paid images out of `_unused`, and the renaming of leftover lowercase slugs. A dash means the file no longer exists (a byte identical duplicate that was removed, or a file that was never in the repo).

## DAPR-2000--Digital_Audio_Essentials

### Module folders

| Canvas module title | Folder |
|---|---|
| Course Info: Schedule & Outline | `Course_Info__Schedule_and_Outline` |
| Careers: Audio Careers | `Careers__Audio_Careers` |
| Pro Tools: Introduction | `Pro_Tools__Introduction` |
| Sound: The Decibel | `Sound__The_Decibel` |
| Monitoring: Psychoacoustics | `Monitoring__Psychoacoustics` |
| DAW: EQ & Dynamics | `DAW__EQ_and_Dynamics` |
| DAW: Your First Mix | `DAW__Your_First_Mix` |
| Project: Mix 1A - Six of One | `Project__Mix_1A_-_Six_of_One` |
| Processing: Compression | `Processing__Compression` |
| Mixing: Mixing & Mastering | `Mixing__Mixing_and_Mastering` |
| Processing: Time-Based Effects | `Processing__Time-Based_Effects` |
| Project: Mix 1B - Peer Review | `Project__Mix_1B_-_Peer_Review` |
| Signal Flow: Cables & Connections | `Signal_Flow__Cables_and_Connections` |
| Digital Audio: The Digital Domain | `Digital_Audio__The_Digital_Domain` |
| Sound: Wave Properties, Hearing & Frequency | `Sound__Wave_Properties_Hearing_and_Frequency` |
| Project: Mix 2 - Six of One Revision | `Project__Mix_2_-_Six_of_One_Revision` |
| Microphones: Characteristics, Selection & Stereo Techniques | `Microphones__Characteristics_Selection_and_Stereo_Techniques` |
| DAPR 2000: Final Exam | `DAPR_2000__Final_Exam` |
| Bonus: Student Rating of Instructor (SRI) | `Bonus__Student_Rating_of_Instructor_(SRI)` |
| Instructor Use Only (Hidden) | `Instructor_Use_Only_(Hidden)` |
